letting WHITE be 1
letting BLACK be 0
letting WINNER be 1
letting LOOSER be sum([WINNER,1;int(1..)]) % 2
letting STEPS0 be domain int(0..steps)
letting STEPS1 be domain int(1..steps)
letting LENGTH be domain int(1..3)
letting FIELD_STATUS be domain int(0..2)

and([ tickTackToe[0, i, j] = EMPTY | i : LENGTH, j : LENGTH ]),
or([and([ sum([ tickTackToe[(step - 1), row, col] | row : LENGTH, col : LENGTH ]) = sum([ tickTackToe[step, row, col] | row : LENGTH, col : LENGTH ]) | step : STEPS1 ]),and([ (sum([ tickTackToe[(step - 1), row, col] | row : LENGTH, col : LENGTH ]) - sum([step % 2,1;int(1..)])) = sum([ tickTackToe[step, row, col] | row : LENGTH, col : LENGTH ]) | step : STEPS1 ]);int(1..)]),

or([or([or([or([ and([ tickTackToe[steps, row, col] = WINNER | col : LENGTH ]) | row : LENGTH ]),or([ and([ tickTackToe[steps, row, col] = WINNER | row : LENGTH ]) | col : LENGTH ]);int(1..)]),and([ tickTackToe[steps, i, i] = WINNER | i : LENGTH ]);int(1..)]),and([and([tickTackToe[steps, 1, 3] = WINNER,tickTackToe[steps, 2, 2] = WINNER;int(1..)]),tickTackToe[steps, 3, 1] = WINNER;int(1..)]);int(1..)]),
